```
3	import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react'
4	
5	type Suggestion = {
6	  name: string
7	  subtitle: string
8	  lat: number
9	  lng: number
10	}
11	
12	// AP + Telangana bbox: min_lon,min_lat,max_lon,max_lat
13	const BBOX = '76.0,12.0,85.5,20.5'
14	
15	// States we allow in results
16	const ALLOWED_STATES = ['andhra pradesh', 'telangana']
17	
18	// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
19	function parsePhoton(data: any): Suggestion[] {
20	  const seen = new Set<string>()
21	  const results: Suggestion[] = []
22	
23	  for (const feature of data?.features ?? []) {
24	    const p = feature?.properties ?? {}
25	    const coords: [number, number] = feature?.geometry?.coordinates ?? [0, 0]
26	    const lng = coords[0]
27	    const lat = coords[1]
28	
29	    if (!lat || !lng) continue
30	
31	    // Filter to AP + Telangana only
32	    const state: string = (p.state ?? '').toLowerCase()
33	    if (state && !ALLOWED_STATES.some((s) => state.includes(s))) continue
34	
35	    // Best name for this result
36	    const name: string = (
37	      p.name || p.city || p.town || p.village || p.hamlet || p.suburb || p.county || ''
38	    ).trim()
39	    if (!name) continue
40	
41	    const key = name.toLowerCase()
42	    if (seen.has(key)) continue
43	    seen.add(key)
44	
45	    const parts: string[] = []
46	    const district = p.county || p.district || p.state_district
47	    if (district && district.toLowerCase() !== key) parts.push(district)
48	    if (p.state) parts.push(p.state)
49	
50	    results.push({ name, subtitle: parts.join(', '), lat, lng })
51	  }
52	
53	  return results
54	}
55	
56	async function fetchSuggestions(q: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<Suggestion[]> {
57	  const url = new URL('https://photon.komoot.io/api/')
58	  url.searchParams.set('q', q)
59	  url.searchParams.set('limit', '8')
60	  url.searchParams.set('bbox', BBOX)
61	  url.searchParams.set('lang', 'en')
62	
63	  const res = await fetch(url.toString(), { signal })
64	  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`)
65	  const json = await res.json()
66	  return parsePhoton(json)
67	}
68	
69	export default function LocationSearch({
70	  placeholder = 'Search city, town or village... / పట్టణం వెతకండి',
71	  onSelect,
72	  initialValue = '',
73	}: {
74	  placeholder?: string
75	  onSelect: (lat: number, lng: number, name: string) => void
76	  initialValue?: string
77	}) {
78	  const [query, setQuery]             = useState(initialValue)
79	  const [suggestions, setSuggestions] = useState<Suggestion[]>([])
80	  const [loading, setLoading]         = useState(false)
81	  const [showDrop, setShowDrop]       = useState(false)
82	  const [noResults, setNoResults]     = useState(false)
83	  const abortRef     = useRef<AbortController | null>(null)
84	  const timerRef     = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null)
85	  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
86	
87	  const doSearch = async (q: string) => {
88	    abortRef.current?.abort()
89	    const clean = q.trim()
90	    if (clean.length < 2) {
91	      setSuggestions([])
92	      setNoResults(false)
93	      setLoading(false)
94	      setShowDrop(false)
95	      return
96	    }
97	    abortRef.current = new AbortController()
98	    setLoading(true)
99	    setNoResults(false)
100	    try {
101	      const results = await fetchSuggestions(clean, abortRef.current.signal)
102	      setSuggestions(results)
103	      setNoResults(results.length === 0)
104	      setShowDrop(true)
105	    } catch (err: unknown) {
106	      if ((err as Error).name !== 'AbortError') {
107	        setSuggestions([])
108	        setNoResults(true)
109	      }
110	    } finally {
111	      setLoading(false)
112	    }
113	  }
114	
115	  const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
116	    const val = e.target.value
117	    setQuery(val)
118	    if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current)
119	    timerRef.current = setTimeout(() => doSearch(val), 350)
120	    if (val.trim().length < 2) {
121	      setSuggestions([])
122	      setNoResults(false)
123	      setShowDrop(false)
124	    }
125	  }
126	
127	  const handleSelect = (s: Suggestion) => {
128	    setQuery(s.name)
129	    setSuggestions([])
130	    setShowDrop(false)
131	    setNoResults(false)
132	    onSelect(s.lat, s.lng, s.name)
133	  }
134	
135	  const handleClear = () => {
136	    setQuery('')
137	    setSuggestions([])
138	    setShowDrop(false)
139	    setNoResults(false)
140	    abortRef.current?.abort()
141	  }
142	
143	  // Close on outside click
144	  useEffect(() => {
145	    const handler = (e: MouseEvent) => {
146	      if (containerRef.current && !containerRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
147	        setShowDrop(false)
148	      }
149	    }
150	    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handler)
151	    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handler)
152	  }, [])
153	
154	  useEffect(() => () => { abortRef.current?.abort() }, [])
155	
156	  return (
157	    <div ref={containerRef} className="relative">
158	      {/* Input */}
159	      <div className="relative">
160	        <svg
161	          className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 pointer-events-none"
162	          width="17" height="17" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none"
163	          stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round"
164	        >
165	          <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8" />
166	          <path d="m21 21-4.35-4.35" />
167	        </svg>
168	        <input
169	          type="text"
170	          value={query}
171	          onChange={handleChange}
172	          onFocus={() => { if (suggestions.length > 0) setShowDrop(true) }}
173	          placeholder={placeholder}
174	          autoComplete="off"
175	          spellCheck={false}
176	          className="w-full border-2 border-gray-200 rounded-xl pl-9 pr-9 py-3 text-base focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none bg-white"
177	        />
178	        {loading && (
179	          <span className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none">
180	            <span className="animate-spin inline-block w-4 h-4 border-2 border-green-600 border-t-transparent rounded-full" />
181	          </span>
182	        )}
183	        {!loading && query && (
184	          <button
185	            type="button"
186	            onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); handleClear() }}
187	            className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 text-xl leading-none"
188	          >
189	            ×
190	          </button>
191	        )}
192	      </div>
193	
194	      {/* Suggestions dropdown */}
195	      {showDrop && suggestions.length > 0 && (
196	        <div className="absolute z-30 top-full left-0 right-0 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl shadow-xl mt-1 overflow-hidden max-h-64 overflow-y-auto">
197	          {suggestions.map((s, i) => (
198	            <button
199	              key={i}
200	              type="button"
201	              onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); handleSelect(s) }}
202	              className="w-full text-left px-4 py-3 hover:bg-green-50 active:bg-green-100 border-b border-gray-100 last:border-0"
203	            >
204	              <p className="font-semibold text-sm text-gray-900 leading-snug">📍 {s.name}</p>
205	              {s.subtitle && (
206	                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">{s.subtitle}</p>
207	              )}
208	            </button>
209	          ))}
210	        </div>
211	      )}
212	
213	      {/* No results */}
214	      {showDrop && noResults && !loading && (
215	        <div className="absolute z-30 top-full left-0 right-0 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl shadow-xl mt-1 px-4 py-4 text-center">
216	          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-600">
217	            No results / ఫలితాలు లేవు

227	}
```

> AGENT

Now I have a complete picture. Here's what I understand:

---

**The layout context**

`LocationSearch` is used inside a **fixed bottom-sheet modal** on the consumer page (line 743: `fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end`). The input sits near the bottom of the panel. The dropdown is positioned `absolute top-full` — directly **below** the input.

When the mobile keyboard opens:
- On Android Chrome: the layout viewport resizes, the modal shifts up, but the dropdown still opens downward — straight into the keyboard area which is now where the bottom of the modal used to be.
- On iOS Safari: the keyboard **overlays** the page (viewport doesn't resize), fixed elements stay put, and the dropdown goes right behind the keyboard.

**Why `scrollIntoView` won't help here**

The modal is `position: fixed` — it's always in the viewport by definition. Scrolling the page body does nothing for fixed elements. So the standard "scroll into view" approach won't move the modal or the dropdown.

**The right fix: flip the dropdown upward when keyboard is open**

Use `window.visualViewport` resize/scroll events to measure how much space exists below the input within the actual visible area. If there's less than ~220px (not enough for even 3-4 results), flip the dropdown from `top-full mt-1` (opens downward) to `bottom-full mb-1` (opens upward, above the input). The space above the input inside the modal is always available.

This fixes both usages — consumer page bottom sheet AND the farmer dashboard profile modal — since the fix is in the shared `LocationSearch` component.

**Specific changes to `LocationSearch.tsx`:**
1. Add `inputRef` on the `<input>`
2. Add `dropUp` state
3. `useEffect` listening to `visualViewport` resize + scroll — measures space below input, sets `dropUp` when < 220px
4. Dropdown and no-results panel: swap `top-full mt-1` for `bottom-full mb-1` when `dropUp` is true

Shall I go ahead?

```
1	'use client'
2	
3	import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react'
4	
5	type Suggestion = {
6	  name: string
7	  subtitle: string
8	  lat: number
9	  lng: number
10	}
11	
12	// AP + Telangana bbox: min_lon,min_lat,max_lon,max_lat
13	const BBOX = '76.0,12.0,85.5,20.5'
14	
15	// States we allow in results
16	const ALLOWED_STATES = ['andhra pradesh', 'telangana']
17	
18	// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
19	function parsePhoton(data: any): Suggestion[] {
20	  const seen = new Set<string>()
21	  const results: Suggestion[] = []
22	
23	  for (const feature of data?.features ?? []) {
24	    const p = feature?.properties ?? {}
25	    const coords: [number, number] = feature?.geometry?.coordinates ?? [0, 0]
26	    const lng = coords[0]
27	    const lat = coords[1]
28	
29	    if (!lat || !lng) continue
30	
31	    // Filter to AP + Telangana only
32	    const state: string = (p.state ?? '').toLowerCase()
33	    if (state && !ALLOWED_STATES.some((s) => state.includes(s))) continue
34	
35	    // Best name for this result
36	    const name: string = (
37	      p.name || p.city || p.town || p.village || p.hamlet || p.suburb || p.county || ''
38	    ).trim()
39	    if (!name) continue
40	
41	    const key = name.toLowerCase()
42	    if (seen.has(key)) continue
43	    seen.add(key)
44	
45	    const parts: string[] = []
46	    const district = p.county || p.district || p.state_district
47	    if (district && district.toLowerCase() !== key) parts.push(district)
48	    if (p.state) parts.push(p.state)
49	
50	    results.push({ name, subtitle: parts.join(', '), lat, lng })
51	  }
52	
53	  return results
54	}
55	
56	async function fetchSuggestions(q: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<Suggestion[]> {
57	  const url = new URL('https://photon.komoot.io/api/')
58	  url.searchParams.set('q', q)
59	  url.searchParams.set('limit', '8')
60	  url.searchParams.set('bbox', BBOX)
61	  url.searchParams.set('lang', 'en')
62	
63	  const res = await fetch(url.toString(), { signal })
64	  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`)
65	  const json = await res.json()
66	  return parsePhoton(json)
67	}
68	
69	export default function LocationSearch({
70	  placeholder = 'Search city, town or village... / పట్టణం వెతకండి',
71	  onSelect,
72	  initialValue = '',
73	}: {
74	  placeholder?: string
75	  onSelect: (lat: number, lng: number, name: string) => void
76	  initialValue?: string
77	}) {
78	  const [query, setQuery]             = useState(initialValue)
79	  const [suggestions, setSuggestions] = useState<Suggestion[]>([])
80	  const [loading, setLoading]         = useState(false)
81	  const [showDrop, setShowDrop]       = useState(false)
82	  const [noResults, setNoResults]     = useState(false)
83	  const [dropUp, setDropUp]           = useState(false)
84	  const abortRef     = useRef<AbortController | null>(null)
85	  const timerRef     = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null)
86	  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
87	  const inputRef     = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
88	
89	  const doSearch = async (q: string) => {
90	    abortRef.current?.abort()
91	    const clean = q.trim()
92	    if (clean.length < 2) {
93	      setSuggestions([])
94	      setNoResults(false)
95	      setLoading(false)
96	      setShowDrop(false)
97	      return
98	    }
99	    abortRef.current = new AbortController()
100	    setLoading(true)
101	    setNoResults(false)
102	    try {
103	      const results = await fetchSuggestions(clean, abortRef.current.signal)
104	      setSuggestions(results)
105	      setNoResults(results.length === 0)
106	      setShowDrop(true)
107	    } catch (err: unknown) {
108	      if ((err as Error).name !== 'AbortError') {
109	        setSuggestions([])
110	        setNoResults(true)
111	      }
112	    } finally {
113	      setLoading(false)
114	    }
115	  }
116	
117	  const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
118	    const val = e.target.value
119	    setQuery(val)
120	    if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current)
121	    timerRef.current = setTimeout(() => doSearch(val), 350)
122	    if (val.trim().length < 2) {
123	      setSuggestions([])
124	      setNoResults(false)
125	      setShowDrop(false)
126	    }
127	  }
128	
129	  const handleSelect = (s: Suggestion) => {
130	    setQuery(s.name)
131	    setSuggestions([])
132	    setShowDrop(false)
133	    setNoResults(false)
134	    onSelect(s.lat, s.lng, s.name)
135	  }
136	
137	  const handleClear = () => {
138	    setQuery('')
139	    setSuggestions([])
140	    setShowDrop(false)
141	    setNoResults(false)
142	    abortRef.current?.abort()
143	  }
144	
145	  // Flip dropdown upward when the keyboard shrinks the visual viewport and
146	  // leaves insufficient space below the input to show results
147	  useEffect(() => {
148	    const vv = window.visualViewport
149	    if (!vv) return
150	    const update = () => {
151	      if (!inputRef.current) return
152	      const rect = inputRef.current.getBoundingClientRect()
153	      // Pixels available between the bottom of the input and the top of the keyboard
154	      const spaceBelow = vv.height + vv.offsetTop - rect.bottom
155	      setDropUp(spaceBelow < 220)
156	    }
157	    vv.addEventListener('resize', update)
158	    vv.addEventListener('scroll', update)
159	    return () => {
160	      vv.removeEventListener('resize', update)
161	      vv.removeEventListener('scroll', update)
162	    }
163	  }, [])
164	
165	  // Close on outside click
166	  useEffect(() => {
167	    const handler = (e: MouseEvent) => {
168	      if (containerRef.current && !containerRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
169	        setShowDrop(false)
170	      }
171	    }
172	    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handler)
173	    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handler)
174	  }, [])
175	
176	  useEffect(() => () => { abortRef.current?.abort() }, [])
177	
178	  return (
179	    <div ref={containerRef} className="relative">
180	      {/* Input */}
181	      <div className="relative">
182	        <svg
183	          className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 pointer-events-none"
184	          width="17" height="17" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none"
185	          stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round"
186	        >
187	          <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8" />
188	          <path d="m21 21-4.35-4.35" />
189	        </svg>
190	        <input
191	          ref={inputRef}
192	          type="text"
193	          value={query}
194	          onChange={handleChange}
195	          onFocus={() => { if (suggestions.length > 0) setShowDrop(true) }}
196	          placeholder={placeholder}
197	          autoComplete="off"
198	          spellCheck={false}
199	          className="w-full border-2 border-gray-200 rounded-xl pl-9 pr-9 py-3 text-base focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none bg-white"
200	        />
201	        {loading && (
202	          <span className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none">
203	            <span className="animate-spin inline-block w-4 h-4 border-2 border-green-600 border-t-transparent rounded-full" />
204	          </span>
205	        )}
206	        {!loading && query && (
207	          <button
208	            type="button"
209	            onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); handleClear() }}
210	            className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 text-xl leading-none"
211	          >
212	            ×
213	          </button>
214	        )}
215	      </div>
216	
217	      {/* Suggestions dropdown */}
218	      {showDrop && suggestions.length > 0 && (
219	        <div className={`absolute z-30 left-0 right-0 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl shadow-xl overflow-hidden max-h-64 overflow-y-auto ${dropUp ? 'bottom-full mb-1' : 'top-full mt-1'}`}>
220	          {suggestions.map((s, i) => (
221	            <button
222	              key={i}
223	              type="button"
224	              onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); handleSelect(s) }}
225	              className="w-full text-left px-4 py-3 hover:bg-green-50 active:bg-green-100 border-b border-gray-100 last:border-0"
226	            >
227	              <p className="font-semibold text-sm text-gray-900 leading-snug">📍 {s.name}</p>
228	              {s.subtitle && (
229	                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">{s.subtitle}</p>
230	              )}
231	            </button>
232	          ))}
233	        </div>
234	      )}
235	
236	      {/* No results */}
237	      {showDrop && noResults && !loading && (
238	        <div className={`absolute z-30 left-0 right-0 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl shadow-xl px-4 py-4 text-center ${dropUp ? 'bottom-full mb-1' : 'top-full mt-1'}`}>
239	          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-600">
240	            No results / ఫలితాలు లేవు
241	          </p>
242	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1 leading-snug">
243	            Try the full name or a nearby larger town<br />
244	            పూర్తి పేరు లేదా దగ్గరలోని పెద్ద పట్టణం వెతకండి
245	          </p>
246	        </div>
247	      )}
248	    </div>
249	  )
250	}
251
```

> AGENT

Clean build. Here's what changed and why each piece matters:

**`dropUp` state** — tracks whether to open the dropdown upward or downward. Starts `false` (opens down by default).

**`inputRef`** — attached to the `<input>` so the effect can measure its position in the viewport at any moment.

**`visualViewport` effect** — fires on every `resize` and `scroll` of the visual viewport (both happen when the software keyboard opens/closes or the user scrolls). It reads:
```
spaceBelow = vv.height + vv.offsetTop - rect.bottom
```
`vv.height` is the keyboard-excluded visible height. `vv.offsetTop` accounts for any viewport panning (iOS Safari can pan the viewport). `rect.bottom` is where the input ends in screen coordinates. If the gap left below the input is under 220px — not enough to comfortably show even 3 results — it sets `dropUp = true`.

**Dropdown and no-results panel** — both swap `top-full mt-1` for `bottom-full mb-1` when `dropUp` is true. `bottom-full` positions the panel flush with the top edge of the input, growing upward. The `max-h-64` cap (256px) keeps it within the modal space above the input.

This fixes both usages automatically — the consumer page location bottom sheet and the farmer dashboard profile modal — since the fix lives in the shared component.

- Default `paymentMethod` switched from `'cod'` to `'upi'` — cart opens with UPI selected.
- Added `showMorePayment` state (default `false`).

- Tapping that link sets `showMorePayment = true`, which swaps the link for the COD button. From there the COD button behaves exactly as before.

```
16	export default function ReviewsTab({

33	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
34	  const [done, setDone] = useState(false)
35	
36	  const avg =
37	    reviews.length > 0
38	      ? reviews.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.star_rating, 0) / reviews.length
39	      : 0
40	
41	  const counts = [5, 4, 3, 2, 1].map((star) => ({
42	    star,
43	    count: reviews.filter((r) => r.star_rating === star).length,
44	  }))
45	
46	  const resetForm = () => {
47	    setName('')
48	    setPhone('')
49	    setRating(0)
50	    setHoverRating(0)
51	    setReviewText('')
52	    setProduceBought('')
53	    setError('')
54	  }
55	
56	  const handleSubmit = async () => {
57	    if (!name.trim() || phone.replace(/\D/g, '').length < 10 || rating === 0) {
58	      setError(tx.reviewRequiredFields)
59	      return
60	    }
61	    setSubmitting(true)
62	    setError('')
63	
64	    const res = await fetch('/api/reviews', {

71	        review_text: reviewText.trim() || null,
72	        produce_ordered: produceBought.trim() || null,
73	      }),
74	    })
75	
76	    const json = await res.json().catch(() => ({}))
77	    setSubmitting(false)
78	
79	    if (!res.ok) {
80	      setError(json.error ?? tx.reviewError)
81	      return
82	    }
83	
84	    if (json.review) setReviews((prev) => [json.review as Review, ...prev])
85	    setDone(true)
86	    setShowForm(false)
87	    resetForm()
88	  }
89	
90	  return (
91	    <div className="space-y-4">

95	          <div className="text-3xl mb-1">⭐</div>
96	          <p className="font-bold text-green-800 text-sm">{tx.reviewSubmitted}</p>
97	        </div>

102	        >

104	        </button>
105	      ) : (
106	        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
107	          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-100 bg-gray-50">
108	            <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-sm">{tx.writeReview}</h3>
109	            <button
110	              onClick={() => { setShowForm(false); resetForm() }}
111	              className="text-gray-400 text-2xl leading-none p-1"
112	            >
113	              ×
114	            </button>
115	          </div>
116	
117	          <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
118	            {/* Star picker */}
119	            <div>
120	              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-2">
121	                {tx.starRatingLabel}
122	              </label>
123	              <div className="flex gap-1">
124	                {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((s) => (
125	                  <button
126	                    key={s}
127	                    onClick={() => setRating(s)}
128	                    onMouseEnter={() => setHoverRating(s)}
129	                    onMouseLeave={() => setHoverRating(0)}
130	                    className="text-4xl leading-none transition-transform active:scale-110"
131	                    aria-label={`${s} stars`}
132	                  >
133	                    <span className={(hoverRating || rating) >= s ? 'text-yellow-400' : 'text-gray-200'}>
134	                      ★
135	                    </span>
136	                  </button>
137	                ))}
138	              </div>
139	            </div>
140	
141	            <div>
142	              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1.5">
143	                {tx.reviewerNameLabel}
144	              </label>
145	              <input
146	                type="text"
147	                value={name}
148	                onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
149	                placeholder="Ramya"
150	                className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
151	              />
152	            </div>
153	
154	            <div>
155	              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1.5">
156	                {tx.reviewerPhoneLabel}
157	              </label>
158	              <div className="flex gap-2">
159	                <span className="flex items-center px-3 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-gray-600 font-medium">
160	                  +91
161	                </span>
162	                <input

164	                  inputMode="numeric"
165	                  value={phone}

167	                  placeholder="9876543210"
168	                  maxLength={10}
169	                  className="flex-1 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
170	                />
171	              </div>
172	              <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 mt-1">Used only to verify your review. / సమీక్షను ధృవీకరించడానికి మాత్రమే.</p>

177	                {tx.produceOrderedLabel}

185	              />

188	            <div>
189	              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1.5">
190	                {tx.reviewTextLabel}
191	              </label>
192	              <textarea
193	                value={reviewText}
194	                onChange={(e) => setReviewText(e.target.value.slice(0, 400))}
195	                placeholder="Share your experience with this farmer..."
196	                rows={3}
197	                className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm resize-none focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
198	              />
199	              <p className="text-right text-xs text-gray-400">{reviewText.length}/400</p>
200	            </div>
201	
202	            {error && (
203	              <p className="text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2">{error}</p>
204	            )}
205	
206	            <div className="flex gap-3">
207	              <button
208	                onClick={() => { setShowForm(false); resetForm() }}
209	                className="flex-1 border-2 border-gray-200 text-gray-600 font-semibold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
210	              >

217	              >

219	              </button>

228	          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">

234	                    ★

248	                    />
```

```
1	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
2	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
3	
4	export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
5	  try {
6	    const body = await req.json()
7	    const { farmer_id, reviewer_name, star_rating, review_text, produce_ordered } = body
8	
9	    if (!farmer_id || !reviewer_name?.trim() || !star_rating) {
10	      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing required fields' }, { status: 400 })
11	    }
12	    if (star_rating < 1 || star_rating > 5) {
13	      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid rating' }, { status: 400 })
14	    }
15	
16	    const supabase = createClient(
17	      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
18	      process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY ?? process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!
19	    )
20	
21	    const { data: review, error } = await supabase
22	      .from('reviews')
23	      .insert({
24	        farmer_id,
25	        reviewer_name: reviewer_name.trim(),
26	        star_rating,
27	        review_text: review_text?.trim() || null,
28	        produce_ordered: produce_ordered?.trim() || null,
29	        approved: true,
30	      })
31	      .select('*')
32	      .single()
33	
34	    if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
35	
36	    // Recalculate rating_avg and buyer_count from all approved reviews
37	    const { data: allReviews } = await supabase
38	      .from('reviews')
39	      .select('star_rating')
40	      .eq('farmer_id', farmer_id)
41	      .eq('approved', true)
42	
43	    if (allReviews && allReviews.length > 0) {
44	      const avg =
45	        allReviews.reduce((sum: number, r: { star_rating: number }) => sum + r.star_rating, 0) /
46	        allReviews.length
47	      await supabase
48	        .from('farmers')
49	        .update({
50	          rating_avg: Math.round(avg * 10) / 10,
51	          buyer_count: allReviews.length,
52	        })
53	        .eq('id', farmer_id)
54	    }
55	
56	    return NextResponse.json({ review })
57	  } catch {
58	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Internal server error' }, { status: 500 })
59	  }
60	}
61
```

> AGENT

The phone isn't stored in DB at all. I'll implement a two-layer check: **localStorage** (device-level gate, works immediately) + **server-side** query against `reviewer_phone` column. If the column doesn't exist yet I'll add it safely via the insert — Supabase will just ignore unknown columns if not present, so I'll handle both paths.

**Client** (`ReviewsTab.tsx`):
- On mount: read `yff_reviewed_<farmerId>` from localStorage → if set, skip straight to the "already reviewed" state
- After success: write the key

**API** (`route.ts`):
- Accept `reviewer_phone` in body, normalize it to 10 digits
- Query `reviews` for same `farmer_id` + `reviewer_phone` — if a row exists, return 409
- Include `reviewer_phone` in the insert

```json
  "old_string": "export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  try {\n    const body = await req.json()\n    const { farmer_id, reviewer_name, star_rating, review_text, produce_ordered } = body\n\n    if (!farmer_id || !reviewer_name?.trim() || !star_rating) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing required fields' }, { status: 400 })\n    }\n    if (star_rating < 1 || star_rating > 5) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid rating' }, { status: 400 })\n    }\n\n    const supabase = createClient(\n      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n      process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY ?? process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!\n    )\n\n    const { data: review, error } = await supabase\n      .from('reviews')\n      .insert({\n        farmer_id,\n        reviewer_name: reviewer_name.trim(),\n        star_rating,\n        review_text: review_text?.trim() || null,\n        produce_ordered: produce_ordered?.trim() || null,\n        approved: true,\n      })\n      .select('*')\n      .single()",
  "new_string": "export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  try {\n    const body = await req.json()\n    const { farmer_id, reviewer_name, reviewer_phone, star_rating, review_text, produce_ordered } = body\n\n    if (!farmer_id || !reviewer_name?.trim() || !star_rating) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing required fields' }, { status: 400 })\n    }\n    if (star_rating < 1 || star_rating > 5) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid rating' }, { status: 400 })\n    }\n\n    const phone = reviewer_phone ? String(reviewer_phone).replace(/\\D/g, '').slice(-10) : null\n\n    const supabase = createClient(\n      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n      process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY ?? process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!\n    )\n\n    // Check for an existing review from the same phone on this farmer\n    if (phone) {\n      const { data: existing } = await supabase\n        .from('reviews')\n        .select('id')\n        .eq('farmer_id', farmer_id)\n        .eq('reviewer_phone', phone)\n        .maybeSingle()\n      if (existing) {\n        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'already_reviewed' }, { status: 409 })\n      }\n    }\n\n    const insertPayload: Record<string, unknown> = {\n      farmer_id,\n      reviewer_name: reviewer_name.trim(),\n      star_rating,\n      review_text: review_text?.trim() || null,\n      produce_ordered: produce_ordered?.trim() || null,\n      approved: true,\n    }\n    if (phone) insertPayload.reviewer_phone = phone\n\n    const { data: review, error } = await supabase\n      .from('reviews')\n      .insert(insertPayload)\n      .select('*')\n      .single()"
}
```

> AGENT

Done. Here's how the one-review-per-consumer rule is enforced at two layers:

**Client (localStorage gate — instant, no network):**
- On tab mount, checks `yff_reviewed_<farmerId>` in localStorage. If found, the "Write a Review" button is replaced with the ⭐ success state immediately — the form never appears.
- On successful submit, writes that key so the same browser/device can't submit again.

**Server (phone-based gate — catches other devices/browsers):**
- `reviewer_phone` (10-digit normalized) is now sent with every submission.
- Before inserting, the API queries `reviews` for the same `farmer_id` + `reviewer_phone`. If a row already exists, it returns `409 already_reviewed` without inserting.
- The client handles the 409 by also writing the localStorage key and showing the success state, so the UI stays consistent.
- `reviewer_phone` is included in the insert payload so future checks work — this requires a `reviewer_phone` column in the `reviews` table. If the column doesn't exist yet you'll need to add it via Supabase dashboard: `ALTER TABLE reviews ADD COLUMN reviewer_phone text;`

> AGENT

Looking at your current codebase and what your friend flagged, here's an honest assessment:

---

**Things already fixed in your code (don't worry about these)**

- Farmer dashboard auth — you have OTP-based login and localStorage only holds the slug for routing, not auth
- Orders written from frontend — your Supabase RLS + service role key pattern handles this reasonably for MVP scale

---

**Things that are real and worth attention before launch**

**1. `/consumer/orders` phone-only lookup — real privacy issue**
Anyone who knows a buyer's phone number can see their order history. Easy fix: add a 4-digit OTP or just show orders only if the phone matches the one stored in the browser's own localStorage.

**2. Zero-price products in UPI flow — real UX bug**
If a farmer lists a product with no price, the UPI screen shows ₹0. A buyer paying ₹0 makes no sense and the farmer gets a confusing WhatsApp. Fix: block "Order & Pay" if total is ₹0, show a message instead.

**3. Screenshot proof for UPI — worth adding eventually**
Since payment verification is manual (farmer checks their UPI app), having the buyer upload a screenshot would reduce disputes. Not launch-blocking but good for trust.

**4. Deployment drift — just redeploy**
If the live site looks behind, it's just a Vercel redeploy. Not a code problem.

---

**What I'd actually prioritize before launch**

1. Fix the `/consumer/orders` privacy issue (30 min of work)
2. Block ₹0 UPI checkout (10 min of work)
3. Redeploy to Vercel

The screenshot proof feature can come after first real orders — you'll learn more from actual farmer feedback than building it speculatively now.

The business model and core flow are sound. These are polish issues, not fundamental problems.

> DEVELOPER

you saying that login for consumers ?

> AGENT

No, not login — that's overkill for this MVP.

The fix is simpler: when a buyer submits their phone number on `/consumer/orders`, only show orders that are already stored in **that browser's own localStorage** (from when they placed the order). No OTP, no account, no login.

Right now the page queries Supabase directly with just the phone number — anyone who guesses or knows someone's number can see their orders.

The fix: cross-reference the phone input against the `yff_consumer_v1` localStorage key that's already written when someone places an order. If the phone matches what's saved locally, show orders. If not, show nothing (or a generic "no orders found").

This way:
- Real buyers see their orders on their own phone/browser automatically
- Random people typing someone else's number get nothing
- Zero friction, zero login, zero OTP

Want me to fix it?

> DEVELOPER

Real buyers see their orders on their own phone/browser automatically. how only real buyers see their orders

> AGENT

Good question. Here's the logic:

When a buyer places an order, the cart already writes their name + phone to `yff_consumer_v1` in localStorage (in `Cart.tsx`). That happens at order time on their device.

So on `/consumer/orders`:
1. Read `yff_consumer_v1` from localStorage → get the phone number
2. Auto-fill and auto-query with that phone — no manual input needed
3. If someone manually types a **different** phone number, still only show results if it matches what's in localStorage

So a real buyer on their own phone → localStorage has their phone → orders load automatically.

A stranger typing someone else's number → their localStorage has a different phone (or none) → query is blocked.

---

**The gap**: if someone clears localStorage or uses a different browser, they lose access to their order history. But for this use case — rural buyers on a single Android browser — that's acceptable. The farmer's WhatsApp message is the real confirmation anyway.

Want me to implement it?

```
106	        fetch('/api/produce', { cache: 'no-store' }),
107	        fetch('/api/produce?status=coming_soon', { cache: 'no-store' }),
108	      ])
109	      const av: ProduceListing[] = await avRes.json().catch(() => [])
110	      const cs: ProduceListing[] = await csRes.json().catch(() => [])
111	      const avArr = Array.isArray(av) ? av : []
112	      const csArr = Array.isArray(cs) ? cs : []
113	      setAvailable(avArr)
114	      setFiltered(avArr)

117	    } catch { /* silent */ }
118	    setLoading(false)
119	  }, [])
120	
121	  useEffect(() => {
122	    fetchData()
123	    const onVisible = () => { if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') fetchData() }
124	    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible)
125	    const channel = supabase
126	      .channel('produce_listings_consumer')
127	      .on('postgres_changes', { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'produce_listings' }, fetchData)
128	      .subscribe()
129	    return () => {
130	      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible)
131	      supabase.removeChannel(channel)
132	    }
133	  }, [fetchData])

136	    if (!search && method === 'all' && category === 'all') {
137	      setFiltered(available)
138	      return
139	    }
140	    searchAbortRef.current?.abort()
141	    searchAbortRef.current = new AbortController()
142	    try {

144	      if (search)              p.set('q', search)
145	      if (method !== 'all')    p.set('method', method)
146	      if (category !== 'all')  p.set('category', category)
147	      const res = await fetch(`/api/produce/search?${p}`, { cache: 'no-store', signal: searchAbortRef.current.signal })
148	      const data = await res.json().catch(() => [])
149	      setFiltered(Array.isArray(data) ? data : [])
150	    } catch (e) {
151	      if ((e as Error).name !== 'AbortError') { /* silent */ }
152	    }
153	  }, [search, method, category, available])
154	
155	  useEffect(() => {
156	    const t = setTimeout(doSearch, 300)
157	    return () => clearTimeout(t)
158	  }, [doSearch])
```

Here's what I understand from your three requests:

**1. Language switching not working**
The "View Farm →" text I just added is hardcoded English — it doesn't use the `tx` translation system, so it won't switch to Telugu. Same issue likely exists on other recently-added text. Need to wire it through the language context with a proper Telugu translation.

**2. Button text**
Change "View Farm →" to something like "View Farmer Profile" (with Telugu equivalent "రైతు ప్రొఫైల్ చూడండి").

**3. Main issue — farmer profile ordering flow**
Currently when a consumer opens a farmer's profile page (`/farmer/[slug]`), they see an "Order on WhatsApp" type button on the produce listings. But you want the ordering experience on the farmer profile to work exactly the same as the consumer page — meaning: add to cart → cart sheet → UPI payment flow. The WhatsApp ordering button on the farmer profile should be removed/replaced with the same "Add to cart" button, so consumers can order the same way regardless of whether they find a product from the browse page or visit the farmer's profile directly.

Is that right? A few things I'd want to confirm:
- Should the farmer profile page share the same cart state as the consumer page (same `useCart` hook / localStorage)?
- Should produce cards on the farmer profile look identical to the consumer page cards, or just functionally the same?

```
56	}) {
57	  const { tx } = useLang()

70	    const { error } = await supabase.from('produce_listings').delete().eq('id', id)

72	      alert(`Could not delete: ${error.message}`)

74	    }
75	    setListings((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== id))
76	  }

87	  }

157	function AddProduceForm({ farmerId, onAdded }: { farmerId: string; onAdded: (item: Produce) => void }) {
158	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
159	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
160	  const [variety, setVariety] = useState('')
161	  const [emoji, setEmoji] = useState('🌿')
162	  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'available' | 'coming_soon'>('available')
163	  const [price, setPrice] = useState('')
164	  const [stock, setStock] = useState('')
165	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
166	  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false)
167	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
168	
169	  const reset = () => {
170	    setName(''); setVariety(''); setEmoji('🌿'); setStatus('available')
171	    setPrice(''); setStock(''); setSuccess(false); setError('')
172	  }
173	
174	  const handleSubmit = async () => {
175	    if (!name.trim()) return
176	    setLoading(true)
177	    setError('')
178	
179	    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
180	      farmer_id: farmerId,
181	      name: name.trim(),
182	      emoji,
183	      status,
184	      method: 'Natural',
185	    }
186	    if (variety.trim()) payload.variety = variety.trim()
187	    if (price) payload.price_tier_1_price = Number(price)
188	    if (stock) payload.stock_qty = Number(stock)
189	
190	    const { data, error: insertError } = await supabase.from('produce_listings').insert(payload).select().single()
191	    setLoading(false)
192	    if (!insertError && data) {
193	      onAdded(data as Produce)
194	      setSuccess(true)
195	      setTimeout(() => { reset(); setOpen(false) }, 1500)
196	    } else {
197	      setError(insertError?.message ?? 'Failed to add produce. Check Supabase RLS policies.')
198	    }
199	  }
200	
201	  if (!open) {
202	    return (
203	      <button
204	        onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
205	        className="w-full border-2 border-dashed border-green-300 rounded-xl py-4 text-green-700 text-sm font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 hover:border-green-500 hover:bg-green-50 transition-colors"
206	      >
207	        <span className="text-lg">+</span> Add your produce / మీ పంట చేర్చండి
208	      </button>
209	    )
210	  }
211	
212	  return (
213	    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-200 p-4 space-y-4">
214	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
215	        <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">Add produce / పంట చేర్చండి</h3>
216	        <button onClick={() => { reset(); setOpen(false) }} className="text-gray-400 text-lg leading-none">×</button>
217	      </div>
218	
219	      {/* Emoji picker */}
220	      <div>
221	        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-2">Pick an icon / ఐకాన్ ఎంచుకోండి</p>
222	        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
223	          {EMOJI_OPTIONS.map((e) => (
224	            <button
225	              key={e}
226	              onClick={() => setEmoji(e)}
227	              className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-lg text-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all ${emoji === e ? 'bg-green-100 ring-2 ring-green-500' : 'bg-gray-50'}`}
228	            >
229	              {e}
230	            </button>
231	          ))}
232	        </div>
233	      </div>
234	
235	      {/* Name + variety */}
236	      <div className="space-y-2">
237	        <input
238	          type="text"
239	          placeholder="Produce name / పంట పేరు (e.g. Papaya)"
240	          value={name}
241	          onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
242	          className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm"
243	        />
244	        <input
245	          type="text"
246	          placeholder="Variety (optional) / రకం"
247	          value={variety}
248	          onChange={(e) => setVariety(e.target.value)}
249	          className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm"
250	        />
251	      </div>
252	
253	      {/* Price + stock */}
254	      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
255	        <input
256	          type="number"
257	          placeholder="Price / kg (₹) / ధర"
258	          value={price}
259	          onChange={(e) => setPrice(e.target.value)}
260	          className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm"
261	        />
262	        <input
263	          type="number"
264	          placeholder="Stock (kg) / నిల్వ"
265	          value={stock}
266	          onChange={(e) => setStock(e.target.value)}
267	          className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm"
268	        />
269	      </div>
270	
271	      {/* Status */}
272	      <div className="flex gap-2">
273	        {STATUS_OPTIONS.map((s) => (
274	          <button
275	            key={s}
276	            onClick={() => setStatus(s as 'available' | 'coming_soon')}
277	            className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors ${
278	              status === s
279	                ? s === 'available' ? 'bg-green-700 text-white' : 'bg-amber-500 text-white'
280	                : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600'
281	            }`}
282	          >
283	            {s === 'available' ? 'Available now / అందుబాటులో' : 'Coming soon / త్వరలో'}
284	          </button>
285	        ))}
286	      </div>
287	
288	      {error && (
289	        <p className="text-center text-xs text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-lg px-3 py-2">{error}</p>
290	      )}
291	
292	      {success ? (
293	        <p className="text-center text-sm text-green-700 font-semibold py-1">
294	          ✓ Added successfully! / చేర్చబడింది!
295	        </p>
296	      ) : (
297	        <button
298	          onClick={handleSubmit}
299	          disabled={loading || !name.trim()}
300	          className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white text-sm font-semibold py-2.5 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50"
301	        >
302	          {loading ? 'Adding... / చేరుస్తోంది' : 'Add produce / పంట చేర్చండి'}
303	        </button>
304	      )}
305	    </div>
306	  )
307	}
308	
309	type FarmerCartInfo = {
310	  farmerId: string
311	  farmerName: string
312	  farmerPhone: string
313	  farmerVillage: string
314	  farmerSlug: string
315	  farmerPickupLocations: string[]
316	  farmerPickupSlots: PickupSlots | null
317	  farmerUpiId?: string
318	}
319	
320	function ProduceCard({
321	  item,
322	  farmerInfo,
323	  onAddToCart,
324	  onDelete,
325	}: {
326	  item: Produce
327	  farmerInfo: FarmerCartInfo
328	  onAddToCart: (qty: number) => void
329	  onDelete?: () => void
330	}) {
331	  const { tx } = useLang()
332	  const [added, setAdded] = useState(false)
333	
334	  const bgColors: Record<string, string> = {
335	    '🍅': 'bg-red-100', '🥬': 'bg-green-100', '🥭': 'bg-orange-100',
336	    '🍆': 'bg-purple-100', '🥕': 'bg-orange-100', '🌽': 'bg-yellow-100',
337	    '🍌': 'bg-yellow-100', '🫑': 'bg-green-100',
338	  }
339	  const emoji = item.emoji ?? '🌿'
340	  const bg = bgColors[emoji] ?? 'bg-green-100'
341	
342	  const handleAdd = () => {
343	    onAddToCart(1)
344	    setAdded(true)
345	    setTimeout(() => setAdded(false), 1500)
346	  }
347	
348	  return (
349	    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
350	      <div className="flex gap-3 p-3">
351	        {item.image_url ? (
352	          <img
353	            src={item.image_url}
354	            alt={item.name}
355	            loading="lazy"
356	            className="rounded-xl w-14 h-14 object-cover flex-shrink-0 bg-gray-100"
357	          />
358	        ) : (
359	          <div className={`${bg} rounded-xl w-14 h-14 flex items-center justify-center text-3xl flex-shrink-0`}>
360	            {emoji}
361	          </div>
362	        )}
363	        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
364	          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
365	            <div>
366	              <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-900 text-sm">{item.name}</h3>
367	              {item.variety && <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{item.variety}</p>}
368	            </div>
369	            {item.stock_qty !== undefined && (
370	              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 flex-shrink-0">{item.stock_qty} {tx.stockLeft}</span>
371	            )}
372	          </div>
373	          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 mt-1.5">
374	            {item.method && (
375	              <span className="bg-green-100 text-green-800 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">{item.method}</span>
376	            )}
377	            {item.pesticide_result && (
378	              <span className="bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">{item.pesticide_result}</span>
379	            )}
380	            {item.brix && (
381	              <span className="bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">BRIX {item.brix}</span>
382	            )}
383	            {item.harvest_date && <FreshnessBadge harvestDate={item.harvest_date} />}
384	          </div>
385	          {item.description && (
386	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-2 leading-snug whitespace-pre-line">
387	              {item.description}
388	            </p>
389	          )}
390	        </div>
391	      </div>
392	
393	      {item.price_tier_1_price && (
394	        <div className="border-t border-gray-100 px-3 py-2 bg-gray-50">
395	          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-xs text-gray-700">
396	            {item.price_tier_1_price && (
397	              <span>{item.price_tier_1_qty ? `1–${item.price_tier_2_qty ? item.price_tier_2_qty - 1 : item.price_tier_1_qty} kg` : 'Per kg'}: <strong>₹{item.price_tier_1_price}</strong></span>
398	            )}
399	            {item.price_tier_2_qty && item.price_tier_2_price && (
400	              <span>{item.price_tier_2_qty}–{item.price_tier_3_qty ? item.price_tier_3_qty - 1 : '+'} kg: <strong>₹{item.price_tier_2_price}</strong></span>
401	            )}
402	            {item.price_tier_3_qty && item.price_tier_3_price && (
403	              <span>{item.price_tier_3_qty}+ kg: <strong>₹{item.price_tier_3_price}</strong></span>
404	            )}
405	          </div>
406	        </div>
407	      )}
408	
409	      <div className="px-3 pb-3 pt-2 space-y-2">
410	        <button
411	          onClick={handleAdd}
412	          className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-2 w-full font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-base transition-colors ${
413	            added
414	              ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
415	              : 'bg-green-700 text-white active:bg-green-800'
416	          }`}
417	        >
418	          {added ? (
419	            <>✓ Added to cart / బుట్టకు చేర్చబడింది</>
420	          ) : (
421	            <>
422	              <span className="text-xl leading-none">+</span>
423	              Add to cart / బుట్టకు చేర్చండి
424	            </>
425	          )}
426	        </button>
427	        {onDelete && (
428	          <button
429	            onClick={onDelete}
430	            className="w-full border border-red-200 text-red-600 font-semibold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-red-50"
431	          >
432	            🗑 Delete / తొలగించు
433	          </button>
434	        )}
435	      </div>
436	    </div>
437	  )
438	}
439	
440	function ComingSoonCard({ item, farmerId, onDelete }: { item: Produce; farmerId: string; onDelete?: () => void }) {
441	  const { tx } = useLang()
442	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
443	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
444	  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false)
445	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
446	
447	  const handleNotify = async () => {
448	    if (!name || !phone) return
449	    setLoading(true)
450	    await supabase.from('notify_requests').insert({
451	      farmer_id: farmerId,
452	      produce_name: item.name,
453	      requester_name: name,
454	      requester_phone: phone,
455	    })
456	    setSubmitted(true)
457	    setLoading(false)
458	  }
459	
460	  return (
461	    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200 p-4">
462	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
463	        <div>
464	          <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-700 text-sm">{item.emoji ?? '🌱'} {item.name}</h3>
465	          {item.available_to && (
466	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
467	              {tx.expected} {new Date(item.available_to).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}
468	            </p>
469	          )}
470	        </div>
471	        <span className="bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full">
472	          {tx.comingSoon}
473	        </span>
474	      </div>
475	
476	      {submitted ? (
477	        <p className="text-sm text-green-700 font-semibold text-center py-2">{tx.notifySuccess}</p>
478	      ) : (
479	        <div className="space-y-2 mt-3">
480	          <input type="text" placeholder={tx.yourName} value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
481	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm" />
482	          <input type="tel" placeholder={tx.yourPhone} value={phone} onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
483	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm" />
484	          <button onClick={handleNotify} disabled={loading}
485	            className="w-full bg-amber-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold py-2.5 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-60">
486	            {loading ? tx.saving : tx.notifyMe}
487	          </button>
488	        </div>
489	      )}
490	
491	      {onDelete && (
492	        <button
493	          onClick={onDelete}
494	          className="mt-3 w-full border border-red-200 text-red-600 font-semibold py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm active:bg-red-50"
495	        >
496	          🗑 Delete / తొలగించు
497	        </button>
498	      )}
499	    </div>
500	  )
501	}
502	
```

```
712	
713	export type TranslationKey = keyof typeof t.en
714	
715	export function bilingual(key: TranslationKey): string {
716	  const en = (t.en as Record<string, string>)[key] ?? ''
717	  const te = (t.te as Record<string, string>)[key] ?? ''
718	  if (!te || en === te) return en
719	  // Long / multi-line text (e.g. WhatsApp templates, descriptions) → stack with separator.
720	  if (en.length > 60 || en.includes('\n') || te.includes('\n')) {
721	    return `${en}\n\n---\n\n${te}`
722	  }
723	  return `${en} / ${te}`
724	}
725
```

```
1	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
2	import { notFound } from 'next/navigation'
3	
4	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
5	import { Suspense } from 'react'
6	import TopNav from '@/components/farmer/TopNav'
7	import FarmCover from '@/components/farmer/FarmCover'
8	import TrustStrip from '@/components/farmer/TrustStrip'
9	import TabSection from '@/components/farmer/TabSection'
10	import StickyBottomBar from '@/components/farmer/StickyBottomBar'
11	
12	export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
13	  const { slug } = await params
14	
15	  const { data: farmer } = await supabase
16	    .from('farmers')
17	    .select('id, name, village, district, story_quote')
18	    .eq('slug', slug)
19	    .single()
20	
21	  if (!farmer) return { title: 'Farmer not found' }
22	
23	  const { data: firstPhoto } = await supabase
24	    .from('media')
25	    .select('url')
26	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
27	    .eq('type', 'photo')
28	    .order('sort_order', { ascending: true })
29	    .limit(1)
30	    .single()
31	
32	  const title = `${farmer.name} — Natural farmer in ${farmer.village}`
33	  const description =
34	    farmer.story_quote ??
35	    `Buy natural produce directly from ${farmer.name}, ${farmer.village}, ${farmer.district}. No middlemen. No chemicals.`
36	
37	  return {
38	    title: `${farmer.name}'s Farm — YourFamilyFarmer`,
39	    description,
40	    openGraph: {
41	      title,
42	      description,
43	      siteName: 'YourFamilyFarmer',
44	      ...(firstPhoto?.url ? { images: [{ url: firstPhoto.url, width: 1200, height: 630, alt: `${farmer.name}'s farm` }] } : {}),
45	    },
46	    twitter: {
47	      card: 'summary_large_image',
48	      title,
49	      description,
50	      ...(firstPhoto?.url ? { images: [firstPhoto.url] } : {}),
51	    },
52	  }
53	}
54	
55	export default async function FarmerPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
56	  const { slug } = await params
57	
58	  const { data: farmer } = await supabase
59	    .from('farmers')
60	    .select('*')
61	    .eq('slug', slug)
62	    .eq('active', true)
63	    .single()
64	
65	  if (!farmer) notFound()
66	
67	  const { data: produce } = await supabase
68	    .from('produce_listings')
69	    .select('*')
70	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
71	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
72	
73	  const { data: reviews } = await supabase
74	    .from('reviews')
75	    .select('*')
76	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
77	    .eq('approved', true)
78	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
79	
80	  const { data: media } = await supabase
81	    .from('media')
82	    .select('*')
83	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
84	    .order('sort_order', { ascending: true })
85	
86	  return (
87	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-24">
88	      <TopNav regionSlug={farmer.region_slug} />
89	      <FarmCover farmer={farmer} />
90	      <TrustStrip farmer={farmer} produceCount={produce?.length ?? 0} />
91	      <Suspense fallback={null}>
92	        <TabSection
93	          farmer={farmer}
94	          produce={produce ?? []}
95	          reviews={reviews ?? []}
96	          media={media ?? []}
97	        />
98	      </Suspense>
99	      <StickyBottomBar farmer={farmer} />
100	    </main>
101	  )
102	}
103
```

```
11	}

13	export type CartItem = {
14	  listingId: string
15	  qty: number
16	  name: string
17	  variety?: string
18	  emoji?: string
19	  pricePerKg?: number
20	  priceTier1Qty?: number
21	  priceTier1Price?: number
22	  priceTier2Qty?: number
23	  priceTier2Price?: number
24	  priceTier3Price?: number
25	  unit?: string
26	  stockQty?: number
27	  farmerId: string
28	  farmerName: string
29	  farmerPhone: string
30	  farmerVillage: string
31	  farmerSlug: string
32	  farmerPickupLocations?: string[]
33	  farmerPickupSlots?: PickupSlots | null
34	  farmerUpiId?: string
35	  farmerQrCodeUrl?: string
36	}
37	
38	export type CartState = Record<string, CartItem>
39	
40	export type ConsumerInfo = { name: string; phone: string }
41	
42	const CART_KEY = 'yff_cart_v1'
43	const CONSUMER_KEY = 'REDACTED'
44	const CART_EVENT = 'yff:cart-change'
45	const UPI_PENDING_KEY = 'yff_upi_pending_v1'
46	const UPI_LAUNCHED_KEY = 'REDACTED'
47	
48	const readCart = (): CartState => {
49	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return {}
50	  try {
51	    const raw = localStorage.getItem(CART_KEY)
52	    return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as CartState) : {}
53	  } catch {
54	    return {}
55	  }
56	}
57	
58	const writeCart = (next: CartState) => {
59	  localStorage.setItem(CART_KEY, JSON.stringify(next))
60	  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(CART_EVENT))
61	}
62	
63	function getActiveTier(qty: number, item: CartItem): { price?: number; isDiscount: boolean } {
64	  const { priceTier1Qty, priceTier1Price, priceTier2Qty, priceTier2Price, priceTier3Price } = item
65	  if (priceTier1Qty == null || priceTier1Price == null)
66	    return { price: item.pricePerKg, isDiscount: false }
67	  if (qty <= priceTier1Qty)
68	    return { price: priceTier1Price, isDiscount: false }
69	  if (priceTier2Qty != null && priceTier2Price != null) {
70	    if (qty <= priceTier2Qty) return { price: priceTier2Price, isDiscount: true }
71	    return { price: priceTier3Price ?? priceTier2Price, isDiscount: true }
72	  }
73	  if (priceTier3Price != null) return { price: priceTier3Price, isDiscount: true }
74	  return { price: priceTier1Price, isDiscount: false }
75	}
76	
77	export function useCart() {
78	  const [cart, setCart] = useState<CartState>({})
79	
80	  useEffect(() => {
81	    setCart(readCart())
82	    const sync = () => setCart(readCart())
83	    window.addEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync)
84	    window.addEventListener('storage', sync)
85	    return () => {
86	      window.removeEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync)
87	      window.removeEventListener('storage', sync)
88	    }
89	  }, [])
90	
91	  const addItem = useCallback((item: Omit<CartItem, 'qty'>, qty = 1) => {
92	    const next = { ...readCart() }
93	    const existing = next[item.listingId]
94	    const rawQty = Math.max(1, (existing?.qty ?? 0) + qty)
95	    const newQty = item.stockQty != null ? Math.min(rawQty, item.stockQty) : rawQty
96	    const merged = { ...item, qty: newQty }
97	    const { price } = getActiveTier(newQty, merged)
98	    next[item.listingId] = { ...merged, pricePerKg: price ?? item.pricePerKg }
99	    writeCart(next)
100	  }, [])
```

```
151	  }, [])
152	
153	  return { info, save }
```

```
53	                isActive
```
